type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n      <span>Signed in as {{ user[\"name\"] }} ({{ user[\"role\"] }})</span>\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n    {% endif %}\n  </header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/login.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p class=\"msg-error\" role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <label for=\"email\">Email</label>\n    <input id=\"email\" name=\"email\" type=\"email\" value=\"{{ email or '' }}\" required autofocus>\n    <label for=\"password\">Password</label>\n    <input id=\"password\" name=\"password\" type=\"password\" required>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Sign in</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/public/home.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T01: sign in and out. Each test names the acceptance criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _login(client, role=\"admin\", password=None, csrf=True):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    data = {\"email\": user[\"email\"], \"password\": password or user[\"password\"]}\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_users_can_log_in_and_reach_admin_home(client):\n    for role, name in ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        c = TestClient(client.app)\n        response = _login(c, role)\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n        home = c.get(\"/admin\")\n        assert home.status_code == 200\n        assert name in home.text  # shell shows who is signed in\n        assert role in home.text\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_is_refused_with_message_and_no_session(client):\n    response = _login(client, password=\"nope\")\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"do not match\" in response.text\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_unknown_email_gets_the_same_message_as_wrong_password(client):\n    token = _csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    unknown = client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"nobody@example.test\",\n                                          \"password\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    wrong = _login(client, password=\"nope\")\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"32a700cb-0df8-4974-ad2f-058f54369d66","timestamp":"2026-10-05T23:22:33.173Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/db.py\n\"\"\"SQLite access. One connection per request; the schema is created on startup.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    name          TEXT NOT NULL,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    created_at    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers under /admin (behind login) and the public site\npreview answers at /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes and are\nincluded here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import db, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth\nfrom app.web import LoginRequired, current_user, render, templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n    app.state.database_path = database_path or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(app.state.database_path)\n\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       https_only=False, max_age=60 * 60 * 12)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    def _login_required(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n        return render(request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}, user=user)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== app/users.py\n\"\"\"Users and passwords. Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so an unknown account and a wrong\n# password take about the same time.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, email: str, name: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown role: {role}\")\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (email, name, password_hash, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (email.strip(), name, hash_password(password), role),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_by_email(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email.strip(),)\n    ).fetchone()\n\n\ndef get_by_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str, password: str\n                 ) -> tuple[sqlite3.Row | None, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Return (user, None) on success, or (None, reason).\n\n    reason is \"invalid\" for an unknown email or wrong password (the caller must\n    show one message for both), or \"inactive\" when the password was right but\n    the account is deactivated.\n    \"\"\"\n    user = get_by_email(conn, email)\n    if user is None:\n        verify_password(_DUMMY_HASH, password)\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not verify_password(user[\"password_hash\"], password):\n        return None, \"invalid\"\n    if not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None, \"inactive\"\n    return user, None\n=== app/web.py\n\"\"\"Shared web plumbing: templates, sessions, the login check and CSRF.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous visitor; the app turns it into a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.database_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active user, or LoginRequired. Guards every admin route.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_by_id(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing form: reject a missing/bad token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not isinstance(sent, str) or not expected\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, name: str, context: dict | None = None,\n           status_code: int = 200, user=None):\n    ctx = {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n           \"user\": user}\n    ctx.update(context or {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, name, ctx, status_code=status_code)\n=== app/routes/auth.py\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.web import get_db, render, verify_csrf\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\nINVALID = \"That email and password do not match. Please try again.\"\nINACTIVE = \"This account has been deactivated. Ask the Head Coach to reactivate it.\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if request.session.get(\"user_id\"):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n    return render(request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Sign in\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef login(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\"),\n          conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    user, reason = users.authenticate(conn, email, password)\n    if user is None:\n        message = INACTIVE if reason == \"inactive\" else INVALID\n        return render(request, \"admin/login.html\",\n                      {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": message, \"email\": email},\n                      status_code=401)\n    # A fresh session on login (new CSRF token too) prevents session fixation.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"role\"] = user[\"role\"]\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef logout(request: Request):\n    request.session.clear()\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db, users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\ndef _csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', response.text)\n    assert match, \"page has no CSRF token\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef database_path(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"A fresh SQLite file with the two demo users, so tests never share state.\"\"\"\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    for role, name in ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        users.create_user(conn, email=DEMO_USERS[role][\"email\"], name=name,\n                          password=DEMO_USERS[role][\"password\"], role=role)\n    conn.close()\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(database_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(database_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n        token = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== templates/admin/hello.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Admin home</h1>\n  <p>Welcome, {{ user[\"name\"] }}.</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/admin/layout.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %} · Admin</title>\n  <style>\n    body { font-family: system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0; color: #222; }\n    header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;\n             gap: 1rem; padding: .75rem 1rem; background: #4b2e83; color: #fff; }\n    header a, header button { color: #fff; }\n    header form { display: inline; margin: 0; }\n    header button { background: none; border: 1px solid #fff; border-radius: 4px;\n                    padding: .25rem .6rem; cursor: pointer; }\n    main { max-width: 40rem; margin: 1.5rem auto; padding: 0 1rem; }\n    label { display: block; margin: .75rem 0 .25rem; }\n    input { width: 100%; padding: .5rem; box-sizing: border-box; }\n    .msg-error { background: #fde8e8; border: 1px solid #c53030; padding: .6rem; }\n    .msg-ok { background: #e6f4ea; border: 1px solid #2f855a; padding: .6rem; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header>\n    <a href=\"/admin\">Kenyon Baseball Alumni · Admin</a>\n    {% if user %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n      <span>Signed in as {{ user[\"name\"] }} ({{ user[\"role\"] }})</span>\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n    {% endif %}\n  </header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/login.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p class=\"msg-error\" role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <label for=\"email\">Email</label>\n    <input id=\"email\" name=\"email\" type=\"email\" value=\"{{ email or '' }}\" required autofocus>\n    <label for=\"password\">Password</label>\n    <input id=\"password\" name=\"password\" type=\"password\" required>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Sign in</button></p>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/public/home.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T01: sign in and out. Each test names the acceptance criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _login(client, role=\"admin\", password=None, csrf=True):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    data = {\"email\": user[\"email\"], \"password\": password or user[\"password\"]}\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_users_can_log_in_and_reach_admin_home(client):\n    for role, name in ((\"admin\", \"Matt Burdette\"), (\"editor\", \"Joe\")):\n        c = TestClient(client.app)\n        response = _login(c, role)\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n        home = c.get(\"/admin\")\n        assert home.status_code == 200\n        assert name in home.text  # shell shows who is signed in\n        assert role in home.text\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_is_refused_with_message_and_no_session(client):\n    response = _login(client, password=\"nope\")\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert \"do not match\" in response.text\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_unknown_email_gets_the_same_message_as_wrong_password(client):\n    token = _csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    unknown = client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"nobody@example.test\",\n                                          \"password\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    wrong = _login(client, password=\"nope\")\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"25556462-2867-40b6-a306-394827666d61","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":2},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"samuelphodges/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2f944cec-7c57-4dec-8fa4-056c472f1065","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"8f6b4904-0a59-4613-99da-a09a06765da7","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"ab2a9ddf-cdfb-4ded-8b83-4045411613e2","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfjx57cfwx5ctS6aYTKNS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Sve9wNJxdFAMfq9MPKkf8i","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t02_pages.py","content":"\"\"\"T02: write, preview and manage pages. Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _create(c, title=\"Alumni Events\", body=\"Hello\", status=\"draft\", slug=\"\", csrf=True):\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"status\": status}\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _page_id(response) -> int:\n    match = re.search(r\"/admin/pages/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert match, response.headers\n    return int(match.group(1))\n\n\ndef _token(c) -> str:\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_signed_in_user_can_create_page_with_generated_slug(client_as, role):\n    \"\"\"Create a page; slug is generated from the title.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(role)\n    response = _create(c, title=\"Alumni Events & More!\", body=\"Welcome\", status=\"published\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    edit = c.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert edit.status_code == 200\n    assert 'value=\"alumni-events-more\"' in edit.text\n    assert \"saved\" in edit.text.lower()  # success message after the redirect\n\n\ndef test_slug_can_be_set_by_the_editor(client_as):\n    \"\"\"The slug can be changed.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = _create(c, title=\"About us\", slug=\"Who We Are\")\n    assert 'value=\"who-we-are\"' in c.get(response.headers[\"location\"]).text\n\n\ndef test_two_pages_cannot_share_a_slug(client_as):\n    \"\"\"The second save shows an error.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert _create(c, title=\"About\").status_code == 303\n    second = _create(c, title=\"Other\", slug=\"about\")\n    assert second.status_code == 409\n    assert \"already\" in second.text.lower()\n    # An error keeps what the editor typed.\n    assert 'value=\"Other\"' in second.text\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_page_may_keep_its_own_slug_but_not_take_another(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    a = _page_id(_create(c, title=\"Alpha\"))\n    _create(c, title=\"Beta\")\n    token = _token(c)\n    same = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{a}\", data={\"title\": \"Alpha 2\", \"slug\": \"alpha\",\n                  \"body\": \"x\", \"status\": \"draft\", \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n    assert same.status_code == 303\n    clash = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{a}\", data={\"title\": \"Alpha 2\", \"slug\": \"beta\",\n                   \"body\": \"x\", \"status\": \"draft\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert clash.status_code == 409\n\n\ndef test_blank_title_is_an_error(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = _create(c, title=\"  \")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert \"title\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_edit_publish_unpublish_delete_each_show_a_message(client_as):\n    \"\"\"A page can be edited, published, unpublished and deleted.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = _page_id(_create(c, title=\"About\"))\n    token = _token(c)\n\n    edited = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}\", data={\"title\": \"About Us\", \"slug\": \"about\",\n                    \"body\": \"New body\", \"status\": \"draft\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert edited.status_code == 200  # redirect followed back to the editor\n    assert \"About Us\" in edited.text and \"saved\" in edited.text.lower()\n\n    pub = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/publish\", data={\"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"published\" in pub.text.lower() and \"About Us\" in pub.text\n    assert re.search(r\"About Us.*?published\", c.get(\"/admin/pages\").text, re.S)\n\n    unpub = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/unpublish\", data={\"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"unpublished\" in unpub.text.lower()\n\n    gone = c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/delete\", data={\"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert \"deleted\" in gone.text.lower()\n    assert \"About Us\" not in gone.text.split(\"<main>\")[1].split(\"deleted\")[-1]\n    assert c.get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\").status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_author_and_timestamps_are_recorded_and_shown(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Author, created and updated timestamps are shown.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = _page_id(_create(c, title=\"About\"))\n    edit = c.get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\").text\n    assert \"Joe\" in edit\n    assert re.search(r\"Created\\s*(<[^>]+>\\s*)*\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", edit)\n    assert re.search(r\"Updated\\s*(<[^>]+>\\s*)*\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", edit)\n\n\ndef test_pages_persist_across_a_restart(client_as, database_path):\n    \"\"\"A fresh app on the same database file still has the page.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    pid = _page_id(_create(c, title=\"Persistent\", body=\"still here\", status=\"published\"))\n    fresh = TestClient(create_app(database_path))\n    # Log in again on the fresh app (sessions are cookies; the secret is shared).\n    fresh.cookies.update(c.cookies)\n    page = fresh.get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\")\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Persistent\" in page.text and \"still here\" in page.text\n\n\ndef test_preview_renders_markdown_and_strips_script_and_onerror(client_as):\n    \"\"\"The preview shows sanitized Markdown.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    body = ('# Big heading\\n\\n<script>alert(1)</script>\\n\\n'\n            '<img src=\"x\" onerror=\"alert(2)\">\\n\\n[ok](https://example.test)')\n    response = c.post(\"/admin/pages/preview\", data={\n        \"title\": \"T\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": body, \"status\": \"draft\",\n        \"csrf_token\": _token(c)})\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    preview = response.text.split('id=\"preview\"')[1]\n    assert \"<h1>Big heading</h1>\" in preview\n    assert \"<script\" not in preview.lower()\n    assert \"onerror\" not in preview.lower()\n    assert \"alert(1)\" not in preview\n    assert \"https://example.test\" in preview\n    # Preview saves nothing.\n    assert \"T\" not in [row for row in re.findall(r\"<td>(.*?)</td>\",\n                                                  c.get(\"/admin/pages\").text)]\n\n\ndef test_markdown_javascript_links_are_stripped_in_preview(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = c.post(\"/admin/pages/preview\", data={\n        \"title\": \"T\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": \"[x](javascript:alert(1))\",\n        \"status\": \"draft\", \"csrf_token\": _token(c)})\n    assert \"javascript:\" not in response.text.split('id=\"preview\"')[1]\n\n\ndef test_content_list_shows_title_status_and_last_update(client_as):\n    \"\"\"A content list shows all pages with title, status and last update.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    _create(c, title=\"Draft one\", status=\"draft\")\n    _create(c, title=\"Live one\", status=\"published\")\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/pages\").text\n    assert \"Draft one\" in listing and \"Live one\" in listing\n    assert \"draft\" in listing and \"published\" in listing\n    assert re.search(r\"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", listing)\n\n\ndef _admin_page_routes(app):\n    from fastapi.routing import APIRoute\n    return [r for r in app.routes\n            if isinstance(r, APIRoute) and r.path.startswith(\"/admin/pages\")]\n\n\ndef test_every_page_form_rejects_a_post_without_csrf(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Each state-changing page route rejects a POST without a token.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = _page_id(_create(c, title=\"About\"))\n    posts = [(\"/admin/pages\", {}), (f\"/admin/pages/{pid}\", {}),\n             (f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/publish\", {}), (f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/unpublish\", {}),\n             (f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/delete\", {}), (\"/admin/pages/preview\", {})]\n    form = {\"title\": \"x\", \"slug\": \"x2\", \"body\": \"b\", \"status\": \"draft\"}\n    for path, _ in posts:\n        assert c.post(path, data=form).status_code == 403, path\n        assert c.post(path, data={**form, \"csrf_token\": \"wrong\"}).status_code == 403, path\n    # And nothing changed.\n    assert c.get(f\"/admin/pages/{pid}/edit\").status_code == 200\n    assert len(re.findall(r\"/edit\", c.get(\"/admin/pages\").text)) >= 1\n\n\ndef test_every_post_route_is_covered_by_the_csrf_test(client_as):\n    \"\"\"Guard: a new POST route under /admin/pages must be added to the test above.\"\"\"\n    paths = {r.path for r in _admin_page_routes(client_as(\"editor\").app)\n             if \"POST\" in r.methods}\n    assert paths == {\"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/pages/{page_id}\",\n test_anonymous_visitor_is_redirected_to_login_on_every_page_route(client):\n    \"\"\"Routes come from the app's router, so a later route cannot escape.\"\"\"\n    routes = _admin_page_routes(client.app)\n    assert routes\n    for route in routes:\n        path = route.path.replace(\"{page_id}\", \"1\")\n        for method in route.methods - {\"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}:\n            response = client.request(method, path, follow_redirects=False)\n            assert response.status_code == 303, (method, path)\n            assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\", (method, path)\n\n\ndef test_unknown_page_is_404(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/pages/999/edit\").status_code == Each test names the criterion it covers.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import _csrf_from\n\n\ndef _create(c, title=\"Alumni Events\", body=\"Hello\", status=\"draft\", slug=\"\", csrf=True):\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"status\": status}\n    if csrf:\n        data[\"csrf_token\"] = _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef _page_id(response) -> int:\n    match = re.search(r\"/admin/pages/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert match, response.headers\n    return int(match.group(1))\n\n\ndef _token(c) -> str:\n    return _csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin/pages/new\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_signed_in_user_can_create_page_with_generated_slug(client_as, role):\n    \"\"\"Create a page; slug is generated from the title.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(role)\n    response = _create(c, title=\"Alumni Events & More!\", body=\"Welcome\", status=\"published\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    edit = c.get(response.headers[\"location\"])\n    assert edit.status_code == 200\n    assert 'value=\"alumni-events-more\"' in edit.text\n    assert \"saved\" in edit.text.lower()  # success message after the redirect\n\n\ndef test_slug_can_be_set_by_the_editor(client_as):\n    \"\"\"The slug can be changed.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = \"\"\"The second save shows an error.\"\"\"\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert _create(c, title=\"About\").status_code == 303\n    second = _create(c, title=\"Other\", slug=\"about\")\n    assert second.status_code == 409\n    assert \"already\" in second.text.lower()\n    # An error keeps what the editor typed.\n    assert 'value=\"Other\"' in second.text\n\n\ndef \"published\")\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT pages.*, users.name AS author_name FROM pages \"\n           \"JOIN users ON users.id = pages.author_id\")\n\n\nclass SlugTaken(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    ascii_text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", ascii_text.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY pages.updated_at DESC, pages.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef get(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE pages.id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef create(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n           status: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    now = _now()\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, slug, body, status, author_id, now, now))\n        conn.commit()\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        conn.rollback()\n        raise SlugTaken(slug) from exc\n    assert cur.lastrowid is not None\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, *, title: str, slug: str,\n           body: str, status: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?\"\n            \" WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body, status, _now(), page_id))\n        conn.commit()\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        conn.rollback()\n        raise SlugTaken(slug) from exc\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, status: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (status, _now(), page_id))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef delete(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", flash, get_db, render, verify_csrf\n\n# Every route here needs a signed-in user; every POST also needs a CSRF token.\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such page.\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _back_to_editor(page_id: int) -> RedirectResponse:\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}/edit\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _editor(request: Request, user, form: dict, page: sqlite3.Row | None = None,\n            status_code: int = 200, preview: page\" if page else \"New page\", \"form\": form,\n                   \"page\": page, \"preview\": preview}, status_code=status_code, user=user)\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str, status: str) -> tuple[dict, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalise the submitted fields; return (fields, error).\"\"\"\n    title = title.strip()\n    fields = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": pages.slugify(slug or title), \"body\": body,\n              \"status\": status if status in pages.STATUSES else \"draft\"}\n    if not title:\n        return fields, \"A page needs a title.\"\n    if not fields[\"slug\"]:\n        return fields, \"The slug needs at least one letter or number.\"\n    return fields, None\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)):\n    return render(request, \"admin/page_list.html\",\n                  {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"pages\": pages.list_pages(conn)}, user=user)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_page(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, {\"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body\": \"\", \"status\": \"draft\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=post)\ndef preview(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"),\n            body: str = slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    ascii_text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", {"parentUuid":"c9971d75-bad2-4527-b1be-ff84ac4ef9d1","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"973449ad-6453-4745-a0a1-d2207f0c7ce3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/sphod/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Review the uncommitted changes (since HEAD) against issue #3 (T02) and spec #1. Report findings only."}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"bd340584-402d-4934-9402-758213ff687e","timestamp":"2026-10-05T23:24:07.609Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01TWH46TwhTthkycVpZpBiyk","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"8f6b4904-0a59-4613-99da-a09a06765da7","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The report follows:\n  STANDARDS review (read-only)\n  \n  (a) Documented-standard violations\n  \n  HARD: none found.\n  - CSRF: every POST form (page_edit.html, page_list.html x2, layout logout) carries csrf_token. Routes use verify_csrf, and the test file guards route coverage.\n  - Markdown: render_markdown uses nh3.clean. The `{{ preview | safe }}` in page_edit.html is safe because the preview is sanitized first.\n  - No new dependencies. markdown-it-py and nh3 are already in pyproject.toml.\n  - Drafts: nothing here touches site/.\n  - Relative paths: the \"/admin/...\" hrefs are admin templates, not published HTML, so the rule does not apply.\n  \n  JUDGEMENT / soft\n  1. Vocabulary (CONTEXT.md \"use the glossary\"):\n     - The code uses \"page\" and \"Pages\" for every content item. CONTEXT.md defines \"Post Type\" (Event / Team Update), \"Event\" and \"Team Update\", and uses \"page\" only for fixed sections such as Team Schedule and Home. The glossary has no generic \"Page\" term, so either add one or use \"post\".\n     - The admin UI uses \"Publish\" and \"Unpublish\" as a per-item status toggle. The glossary defines **Publish** as the explicit step that turns published content into the public site, and says edits are not live until it runs. The button label collides with that definition. Resolve it in the glossary, for example by calling the toggle \"Mark published\".\n     - **Locked** (Head Coach only) is not handled in the new routes. This is probably a later ticket.\n  2. Hunk in app/routes/pages.py: the ticket/spec for T02 should be checked for whether Post Type is required here. This is a spec-axis question, so it is only noted.\n  \n  (b) Baseline smells (judgement)\n  \n  - Duplicated Code, app/routes/pages.py, `create_page` and `save_page`:\n    `fields, error = _clean(...)`, then the `try`/`except pages.SlugTaken`, then `_editor(..., status_code=409, error=f\"The slug “{fields['slug']}” is already used by another page.\")`, then `flash(...saved.)` and `_back_to_editor`. The error string and the save flow are copied verbatim.\n    - `publish_page`, `unpublish_page` and `delete_page` also repeat the same `_page_or_404`, action, `flash`, `RedirectResponse(\"/admin/pages\")` skeleton.\n    - In page_list.html the Publish/Unpublish toggle repeats the ternary `'unpublish' if p.status == 'published' else 'publish'` twice.\n  - Primitive Obsession / Data Clumps:\n    - `title, slug, body, status` travel together as loose kwargs and dicts through `_clean`, `_editor`, `pages.create`, `pages.update`, `preview`, and the route signatures.\n    - `status` is a bare string. `\"published\"` and `\"draft\"` literals appear in routes/pages.py, in `STATUSES`, in the SQL CHECK, and as a hardcoded `('draft', 'published')` tuple in page_edit.html. That is Repeated Switches / Shotgun Surgery risk when a status is added.\n  - Mysterious Name:\n    - `post = [Depends(verify_csrf)]` is a module-level name that reads like a verb or noun. Something like `csrf_required` would be clearer.\n    - `_editor(...)` returns a response, so the name is unclear.\n    - The `page_id: str` form field in `preview` exists only to carry hidden state.\n  - Feature Envy / Message Chains: minor. `_editor` calls `flash` as a side effect of rendering, and render pops it again. Showing an error through the session flash on the same response is a roundabout path.\n  - Divergent Change: app/pages.py combines slugify, timestamp generation, and SQL. These are small now and not a violation.\n  - Speculative Generality: none significant. app/web.py `flash(kind=\"ok\")` has a default for two kinds, which is fine.\n  - Test smell (not a baseline item): in tests/test_t02_pages.py the `posts` list in `test_every_page_form_rejects_a_post_without_csrf` carries unused `{}` tuple members, and the route-set guard test hardcodes the paths a second time.\n  \n  Files reviewed: app/db.py, app/main.py, app/web.py, app/markdown.py, app/pages.py, app/routes/pages.py, templates/admin/{layout,page_edit,page_list}.html, tests/test_t02_pages.py.\n</agent-message>"}

The report follows:\n  SPEC review of T02 (pages), working tree vs HEAD.\n  \n  Acceptance criteria check (all 9 are implemented and have a test):\n  1. Create with slug generated and editable: met (test_signed_in_user_can_create_page..., test_slug_can_be_set...).\n  2. Duplicate slug errors: met (409 plus message). Tested on create and edit.\n  3. Edit/publish/unpublish/delete each show a message: met. The test asserts the text of each flash.\n  4. Author, created, updated recorded and shown: met, on the editor only.\n  5. Persists across restart: met. The test builds a fresh app on the same DB file.\n  6. Preview strips script and onerror: met, with nh3 sanitising.\n  7. List shows title, status, last update: met.\n  8. CSRF on every form and rejects a POST without a token: met, with a guard test that enumerates the POST routes.\n  9. Anonymous users redirected to login: met. The test iterates the router's routes.\n  \n  (a) Missing or partial\n  - \"a content list ... filterable by status and type\" (spec capability 5, start only). The list has no status filter. This is arguably T-later, since T02 says only \"a first content list\".\n  - \"Pages ... Same fields and operations as posts\". Edit and delete are POST-only forms in the list, so there is no confirmation fallback without JS. This is minor.\n  - \"every save shows a success or error message\". Preview is not a save, so it is fine. The Save button on a blank title (422) and the duplicate-slug error (409) both show errors. OK.\n  - Test gap: criterion 4 is checked only for the editor view, not for the list's \"last update\". Test gap: the \"editor or admin\" wording is covered for create only, not for edit, publish or delete. Test gap: the CSRF test checks only 403 and does not confirm the data was unchanged after the failed publish or delete. It only checks that the page still exists.\n  \n  (b) Scope creep\n  - `flash()` and the `render()` change in app/web.py are shared infrastructure. They are needed, but they touch T01 code.\n  - Layout CSS changes: `main` widened from 40rem to 52rem, plus a new table/textarea style. The `input` selector was altered, which affects the login form. Minor.\n  - Admin layout nav \"Pages\" link added. This is reasonable, because the spec asks for \"persistent navigation\".\n  - 422 and 409 status codes on form errors. Not asked for, but harmless.\n  - `pages.slugify(slug or title)` re-slugifies a user-typed slug, silently rewriting it (\"Who We Are\" becomes \"who-we-are\"). The spec says the slug \"can be changed\", not that it is normalised.\n  \n  (c) Looks implemented but possibly wrong\n  - Spec: \"Pages persist across a server restart.\" The restart test copies the session cookie to the fresh app. This only proves persistence if the secret key is shared, as the test comment itself says. It passes, so it is probably fine.\n  - Spec: \"Slugs are generated from the title\". A title with no ASCII characters (for example \"日本\") gives an empty slug, which gives the error \"The slug needs at least one letter or number\". That is acceptable, but there is no fallback.\n  - `preview` accepts `page_id` from the form and reads it from the database. It does not validate that the page exists, so a bad id silently previews as new. Minor.\n  - Timestamps are UTC with no timezone label (\"YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS\"). Fine for the spec.\n  - Publish and unpublish redirect to the list, while save redirects to the editor. This is inconsistent but not a spec issue.\n  - Locked pages, the editor/admin split and the Locked flag are not implemented. This is correct, because they belong to other tickets. The `pages` table has no `locked` column, so a later ticket needs a migration (CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS will not add it).\n  \n  No blocking spec failures found.\n</agent-message>"}

{"parentUuid":"731a3e47-d704-4616-adb5-ecad7d537c7c","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfjxFj2fiHjbgvY4aT46A","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Issue #3 is T02 (\"Editors can write, preview and manage pages\"), so I built that. It's committed as `af5d094` with `Closes #3`. The full suite passes (51 tests) and mypy is clean. I haven't run the app in a browser. If you meant T03 (publish and deploy), that is issue #4.\n\n- **What it does:** pages now have a database table, a sanitized Markdown preview, and create, edit, publish, unpublish and delete with a success or error message each time. There is a page list and a \"Pages\" link in the admin nav. Every page form carries a CSRF token, and an anonymous visitor is redirected to login on every page route.\n- **Tests:** `tests/test_t02_pages.py` has 16 tests covering all 9 acceptance criteria.\n- **Review:** I ran `/code-review`, posted the findings and how I resolved them as a comment on #3, and applied the fixes. {"parentUuid":"42b06617-4149-4e19-8f5c-cd9f8d8c3314","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfjxVXLMNP2bMTdp7VZQa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Yes, with one caveat about how I got there.\n\nThe manual's per-ticket runbook (§6.3) says to drive `/tdd` (a failing test, then code, then a green test), run `/code-review`, post the findings as a comment, then commit with `Closes #N`. Where this ticket stands against that:\n\n- **Green:** the full suite passes, 51 tests, including the 16 new ones in `tests/test_t02_pages.py`. mypy is clean.\n- **Code review:** it ran and I posted the findings and their resolutions as a comment on #3 before committing.\n- **Commit:** it starts `T02:` and ends `Closes #3`.\n- **On GitHub:** `gh issue view 3` shows the issue as CLOSED with one comment, and the commit is pushed.\n\nThe caveat is that I wrote the tests first but never ran them red before writing the code. I wrote the whole test file, then the implementation, and the first run was already mostly green. Four tests failed on that run, but only because of bugs in my own test helper and sanitizer settings.
